@GH_active_effect_weight   = 150
@GH_override_effect_weight = 250
# Values of 0 stop the decal from showing up, so this is necessary
@ZERO = 0.004

POD_shapeshift_modifiers = {
	usage = game  

# Shade Form 

	POD_shade_form = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			color = {
				gene = skin_color
				mode = modify
				
				x = -1.0
				y = 1.0
			}			
			color = {
				gene = hair_color
				mode = replace
				x = -1.0
				y = 1.0
			}
		}	
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 501
				GH_must_use_statue_effect_shadow = yes
			}
		}	
	}

# Manticores

                value = 1.0
            }

				mode = replace
				gene = POD_fangs_accessory
				template = default_fangs
				value = 1.0 
			}

                mode = modify

                template = no_eyes
                value = 0.5 #1.0
            }
            accessory = {
                mode = add
                gene = beards
                template = no_beard
                value = 1.0
            }							
			accessory = {
				mode = add				
				gene = headwear_custom_1
				template = skeleton_head
				value = 0.25
			}

	POD_skull_jwls = {
		dna_modifiers = {
			morph = {
				mode = replace				
				gene = gene_bs_body_type
				template = no_portrait
				value = 1.0
			}
            morph = {
                mode = modify			
                gene = complexion
                template = complexion_no_face
                value = 1.0
            }
            morph = {
                mode = replace				
                gene = gene_age
                template = no_aging
                value = 0
            }
            accessory = {
                mode = add
                gene = hairstyles
				template = no_hairstyles
				value = 0
            }			
			morph = {
				mode = modify
				gene = gene_eyebrows_shape
				template = no_eyebrows
				value = 0.5
			}
			morph = {
				mode = modify
				gene = gene_eyebrows_fullness
				template = no_eyebrows
				value = 0.5
			}
            accessory = {
				mode = replace
				gene = eyelashes_accessory
				template = no_eyelashes
				value = 0.5
			}			
            accessory = {
                mode = modify		
                gene = teeth_accessory
				template = no_teeth
                value = 1.0
            }
			accessory = {
				mode = replace
				gene = POD_fangs_accessory
				template = default_fangs
				value = 1.0 
			}	
			accessory = {
                mode = modify			
                gene = eye_accessory
                template = no_eyes
                value = 0.5 #1.0
            }
            accessory = {
                mode = add
                gene = beards
                template = no_beard
                value = 1.0
            }							
			accessory = {
				mode = add				
				gene = headwear_custom_1
				template = skeleton_head
				value = 0.51
			}
				
		}
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 1200
				POD_skull_jwls_trigger = yes
			}
		}
	}

	POD_normal_eyes = {
		    dna_modifiers = {
			    accessory = {
			    	mode = modify				
			    	gene = eye_accessory
		    		template = normal_eyes
	    			value = 1.0
	     		}
	    	}
		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				POD_portrait_normal_eyes_trigger = yes
			}			
		}
	}

	POD_no_eyes = {
		    dna_modifiers = {
			    accessory = {
			    	mode = modify				
			    	gene = eye_accessory
		    		template = no_eyes
	    			value = 1.0
	     		}
	    	}
		
		weight = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				add = 100
				has_character_flag = POD_no_eyes_flag
			}			
		}
